Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the wind framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 329. The resulting operator achieves bound O(911/n^1).

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_329(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 329. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^911(S, F) is trivial. QED.
